Let's consider exact optimization of Fourier profile in code. Need top-level functions no closures. We can use scipy.optimize.minimize. But need ensure no lambda. Define:

def fourier_f_value(vec, M):
   a = np.asarray(vec).astype(float)
   # maybe clip to [-1.2,1.2]
   c = np.clip(a, -1.2, 1.2)
   x = (np.arange(M)+0.5)/M
   f = np.ones(M)
   for k in range(1,c.size+1): f += c[k-1]*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x)
   minf=f.min()
   pen = 0.0
   if minf < 0:
       pen = 1000.0 * minf*minf
   p = np.maximum(f,0)
   s=p.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12: return 1e9
   p=p/s
   sc=fast_score(p)
   return sc+pen

Then optimize:
def optimize_fourier(M, rng, time_limit, K=4):
   best_vec=None; best_sc=np.inf
   # base starts
   starts = []
   # manual 2-harmonic fills: [-0.6,0.5,0,0...], [-0.7,0.5], [-0.5,0.6], [0.5,-0.5]? etc.
   for ... starts append.
   random: for _ in range(6): [rng.uniform(-0.8,0.8,K)]
   deadline...
   for x0 in starts:
      if time>deadline break
      try:
         res=minimize(fourier_f_value, x0, args=(M,), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':300, 'xatol':1e-5, 'fatol':1e-6, 'adaptive':True})
         if res.success or res.x: evaluate...
      except: pass
   return best_vec,best_sc

Full active Adam code details:
Function active_adam(parent, duration, rng, top_k=64, tau_rel=0.08, lr0=0.1, max_top=512, lr_min=0.01):
   p = normalize_positive(parent)
   n=p.size
   L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   z=log(p); z-=mean; clip
   m,v,b1,b2
   best_p=p; best_s=fast_score(p)
   no_improve=0; step=0
   top_k_current=top_k; tau_current=tau_rel; lr=lr0
   deadline=time.monotonic()+duration
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
      P = np.fft.rfft(p,L)
      c = np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
      c[~finite]=0; c=np.maximum(c,0)
      cm=c.max()
      score=2*n*cm
      if finite and score < best_s - 1e-12: best_s=score; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
      else no_improve +=1
      # every 1000 print? step%2000.
      if no_improve > 4000:
          # perturb from best
          zz = np.log(np.maximum(best_p,1e-300)) + rng.normal(0, 0.2, n)
          zz = np.where(finite, zz,0); zz-=mean; z=clip(zz,-40,40)
          m*=0.1; v*=0.1; no_improve=0
          tau_current = max(0.2, tau_current*0.7?) Actually increase tau_rel? If stuck, broaden: tau_current = min(0.5, tau_current*1.5); top_k_current = min(max_top, int(top_k_current*1.5)); lr = max(lr*0.8,0.02)
          # Also maybe if no_improve very long, try best normalized with noise bigger
      if no_improve > 8000: more aggressive noise sigma 0.4.
      # adapt if improving: maybe tighten tau? if no_improve==0 and step%1000==0: tau_current=max(0.01, tau_current*0.95), top_k_current maybe reduce? Keep.
      # gradient weights
      if top_k_current >= c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size)
      else: part=np.argpartition(c, -top_k_current)[-top_k_current:]; mirror=(2*n-2)-part; idx=np.unique(np.concatenate((part,mirror)))
      vals=c[idx]; maxv=vals.max()
      tau = tau_current*maxv
      if tau <=1e-15: zvals=zeros; w uniform? 
      else: zz=(vals-maxv)/tau; w=np.exp(np.clip(zz,-50,0)); sw=w.sum(); if sw==0: w=uniform else w/=sw
      W=... allocate zeros, set.
      Wref = W[::-1]
      H=irfft(P*rfft(Wref,L),L)
      grad=2*H[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
      # ensure finite
      mean_g=dot(p,grad); grad=grad - p*mean_g
      # Clip extreme
      gmax=np.max(np.abs(grad)); if gmax>1e-2: grad*=1e-2/gmax? But if all grad ~2e-4, clipping not. To avoid noise, clip 0.1. Use 0.05.
      bias +=1
      m=...
      v=...
      update = (m/b1)/(sqrt(v/b2)+1e-8)
      # scale update to have unit RMS? Adam gives ~1. If gradient all zero, update zero.
      nz = np.linalg.norm(update) maybe. To stabilize, if nz>0: update *= (1.0/nz)? This makes step size lr (unequal). Could be too aggressive. Better not; Adam normalizes per coordinate. But global scale depends on gradient magnitude; after first few, m/v ratio ~O(1) regardless? For sparse active, many grad zero -> update zero for inactive, active ~1. OK.
      z = z - lr*update
      z = where finite; z -= mean(z)  # optional to keep scale
      z = clip(z,-40,40)
      p=softmax(z)
      step +=1
      if step%2000==0: print...
   final fast score best_p
   return best_p,best_s

normalize_positive(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); if size 0 None; a=np.where(finite,a,0); a=max(a,0); s=sum; if s<=1e-300 None; return a/s

fast_score(a):
   p=normalize_positive(a); if None inf; n=p.size; L=next_pow2(2n-1); F=rfft(p,L); c=irfft(F*F,L)[:2n-1]; c finite; cm=max(c,0); if cm<=0 inf; return 2n*cm

def active_adam(parent, duration_s, rng, top_k=64, tau_rel=0.06, lr0=0.08, max_top=2048):
    p = normalize_positive(parent)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    n=p.size
    if n<2: return p,2.0?
    L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-40,40)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8; bias=0
    best_p=p.copy(); best_s=fast_score(p); no_improve=0; step=0; last_score=best_s; bad=0
    top_cur=max(16,min(max_top,int(top_k)))
    tau_cur=float(tau_rel)
    lr=float(lr0)
    W=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    old_idx=[0]? Use None. Need reset. Let's implement allocation method simpler. But to reduce overhead, use W and prev_idx array:
       prev_idx = np.array([], dtype=int)
       W_clean? Initially zeros.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
       c = np.fft.irfft(P*P, L)[:2*n-1]
       c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0
       if c.max()<0: c[np.c_]=0? c=np.maximum(c,0)
       cm=float(c.max())
       score=float(2*n*cm)
       if np.isfinite(score):
          if score < best_s - 1e-12:
             best_s=score; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
          else: no_improve +=1
       if step>0 and score > last_score + 1e-12: bad+=1
       else: bad=0
       if bad > 300: lr=max(0.002, lr*0.7); bad=0
       last_score=score
       # adapt no_improve
       if no_improve > 3000:
           noise=0.15 if no_improve<8000 else 0.3
           zz=np.log(np.maximum(best_p,1e-300)) + rng.normal(0.0,noise,n)
           zz=np.where(np.isfinite(zz),zz,0.0); zz-=zz.mean(); z=np.clip(zz,-40,40)
           m*=0.1; v*=0.1; no_improve=0
           tau_cur=min(0.8,tau_cur*1.4)
           top_cur=min(c.size, max(64,int(top_cur*1.5)+16))
           lr=max(0.01, lr*0.8)
       if step > 0 and no_improve == 0 and step % 5000 == 0:
           tau_cur=max(0.015, tau_cur*0.85)
           top_cur=max(32, int(top_cur*0.75))
       # weights
       if top_cur >= c.size:
           idx=np.arange(c.size)
       else:
           part=np.argpartition(c, -int(top_cur))[-int(top_cur):]
           mirror=(2*n-2)-part
           idx=np.unique(np.concatenate((part,mirror)))
       vals=c[idx]
       maxv=float(vals.max())
       if maxv <=0: break? Sequence invalid.
       tau=tau_cur*maxv
       if tau < 1e-15:
           w=np.ones_like(vals)/vals.size
       else:
           zz=(vals-maxv)/tau
           w=np.exp(np.clip(zz,-50,0.0))
           sw=float(w.sum())
           if not finite or sw<=0: w=np.ones_like(vals)/vals.size
           else: w/=sw
       if prev_idx.size: W[prev_idx]=0.0
       W[idx]=w
       prev_idx=idx.copy()  # but if idx new object, W old? Need set old then W idx = w. If idx is sorted unique, okay. But if prev_idx and idx overlap, setting zeros then assign works. If idx not copy? We reset old first.
       Wref = W[::-1]
       H = np.fft.irfft(P * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)
       grad = 2.0 * H[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
       grad[~np.isfinite(grad)] = 0.0
       mean_g = float(np.dot(p, grad))
       grad = grad - p*mean_g
       gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
       if gmax > 0.05:
           grad *= 0.05/gmax
       elif gmax <= 1e-15:
           # no direction, maybe perturb
           z += rng.normal(0.0,0.01,n)
       bias +=1
       m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*grad
       v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*(grad*grad)
       mhat=m/(1-beta1**bias)
       vhat=v/(1-beta2**bias)
       update=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       # optional scale update to avoid huge z: if max |update| > 5, normalize
       umax=float(np.max(np.abs(update)))
       if umax > 3.0: update *= 3.0/umax
       z = z - lr*update
       z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0.0)
       z -= np.mean(z)
       z = np.clip(z, -40.0, 40.0)
       p = softmax_z(z)
       # Additionally ensure p not all tiny? softmax handles.
       step +=1
       if step % 2000 ==0:
           print(f"active step {step} score {score:.6f} best {best_s:.6f} top {int(top_cur)} tau {tau_cur:.3f} lr {lr:.4f}")
    # final score
    p_best=normalize_positive(best_p)
    if p_best is None: p_best=normalize_positive(p)
    s=fast_score(p_best)
    return p_best, float(s)

Now, code for resize_sequence:
def resize_sequence(seq,m):
   p=normalize_positive(seq)
   if p is None: return ones(m)/m
   m=int(m)
   n=p.size
   if n==m: return p.copy()
   if n==1: return np.full(m, 1.0/m)
   x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   x_new=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
   out=np.maximum(out,0)
   s=out.sum()
   if s<=0: return ones(m)/m
   return out/s
If previous sequence length not smooth, linear interpolation fine.

def make_polynomial_seq(coeffs,n):
   x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   u = 2*x -1
   f=ones(n)
   v = u
   for a in coeffs:
       f += a*v
       v *= u*u
   return normalize(clip(f,0))

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else float(kwargs.get('budget_s',2380))
    except: budget=2380

    # pre-polish top candidates

           if dur<10: break

           if p is not None and s<best_s: best_p=p; best_s=s
           rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    # long polish
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem>60:
       dur=rem*0.95
       p,s=active_adam(best_p,dur,rng,top_k=96,tau_rel=0.10,lr0=0.07,max_top=4096)
       if p is not None and s<best_s: best_s=s
       best_p=p
    # optional larger resize

    if rem > 300:
       big=resize_sequence(best_p, 2*N)
       big_s=fast_score(big)
       if big_s < best_s - 1e-9:
          if rem > 900:
             dur=min(300.0, rem-100.0)
             big,big_s=active_adam(big,dur,rng,top_k=96,tau_rel=0.1,lr0=0.05,max_top=2048)
          best_p=big; best_s=big_s
    # final sequence
    if best_p is None: best_p=np.ones(1024)/1024
    seq=np.clip(np.asarray(best_p,float),0,1000)
    # optional ensure sum enough
    if seq.size==0 or seq.sum()<0.01: seq=np.ones(1024)/1024
    # final validation small? maybe call evaluate_sequence on clipped if time_left > 10 and seq.size<=2048 or budget? We can evaluate if seq.size <= 2048 to avoid slow; for 8192 maybe skip.

    return [float(x) for x in seq]

Let's write optimize_fourier:
```
def optimize_fourier(deadline, M=4096, K=4, rng=None):
    out=[]
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
    fixed = [array lengths K]
    starts=[]
    for f in fixed: starts.append(f[:K])
    for i in range(10): starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.35,K).clip(-0.8,0.8))
    best=None; best_s=inf
    for x0 in starts:
       cur=time.monotonic()
       if cur > deadline: break
       s0=fourier_score(x0,M)
       update
       if HAVE_MINIMIZE:
          options={'maxiter':80, 'xatol':1e-5, 'fatol':1e-7, 'adaptive':True}
          # To respect deadline, cannot tell scipy. Limit maxiter small.
          try:
             res=opt.minimize(fourier_score, x0, args=(M,), method='Nelder-Mead', options=options)
             if res.x is not None: update
          except Exception: pass
       out.append(x)
    return out
```

def random_poly_seq(n, rng):

For real p, linear convolution = inverse FFT of P(z)^2 (not P*conj(P)) with zero padding length ≥2n-1. Yes. If we used |P|² that would be cyclic autocorr. So P*F correct. Good. `softmax_z`:
```
def softmax_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if z.size==0: return np.empty(0)
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -50.0)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       return np.full(z.size,1.0/z.size)
    return e/s
```

`fast_conv(n,p)` maybe not needed.

`build_fourier_from_coeffs(coeffs, n, freqs, shift=0.0, grid='mid')`:
```
def make_fourier_seq(coeffs,n,freqs,shift=0.0,grid='mid'):
    c=np.asarray(coeffs,dtype=float)
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)
    if grid=='mid': x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n + shift
    else: x=(np.arange(n))/n + shift
    x=np.mod(x,1.0)
    f=np.ones(n,float)
    for j in range(c.size):
       if abs(c[j])>1e-12:
          f += c[j]*np.sin(2*np.pi*freqs[j]*x)
    return normalize_positive(np.maximum(f,0.0))
```
If shift and grid edge, x≥1 for last? mod fine. But shifting cell centers with mod changes DFT phases; okay.

`fourier_score(vec, M, freqs)`:
```
def fourier_score(vec, M, freqs):
    c=np.asarray(vec,float); c=np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite,c,0), -1.25,1.25)
    x=(np.arange(M)+0.5)/M
    f=np.ones(M)
    for j in range(c.size):
       f += c[j]*np.sin(2*np.pi*freqs[j]*x)
    minf=float(np.min(f))
    pen=0.0
    if minf < -1e-8:
       pen = 200.0 * (minf + 1e-8)**2
    p=np.maximum(f,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return 1e9
    p=p/s
    sc=fast_score(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return 1e9
    return float(sc+pen)
```

def make_poly_seq(coeffs,n):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    u=2*x-1
    f=np.ones(n)
    v=u
    c=np.asarray(coeffs,float)
    for coeff in c:
       f += float(coeff)*v
       v = v * u * u
    return normalize_positive(np.maximum(f,0))
```

Let's code active_adam carefully with local variables.

```
def active_adam(parent, duration_s, rng, top_k=128, tau_rel=0.10, lr0=0.06, max_top=4096):
    p = normalize_positive(parent)
    if p is None: return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
       return p, 2.0
    L = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    c_size = 2*n - 1
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -20.0)
    z -= np.mean(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -40.0, 40.0)
    m = np.zeros(n); v = np.zeros(n)
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8; bias=0
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(p)
    no_improve=0; step=0; lr=float(lr0); tau_cur=float(tau_rel)
    top_limit = int(max_top) if max_top is not None else c_size
    if top_limit <= 0: top_limit = c_size
    if top_limit > c_size: top_limit = c_size
    top_cur = int(min(max(16, int(top_k)), top_limit))
    prev_score = None; bad = 0
    W = np.zeros(c_size)
    prev_idx = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(duration_s))
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
       c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:c_size]
       c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
       c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
       cm = float(c.max())
       score_f = float(2.0 * n * cm)
       if np.isfinite(score_f):
           if score_f < best_s - 1e-12:
               best_s = score_f; best_p = p.copy(); no_improve=0
           else:
               no_improve += 1
           if prev_score is not None and score_f > prev_score + 1e-12:
               bad += 1
           else:
               bad = 0
           if bad > 100:
               lr = max(0.002, lr * 0.75); bad = 0
           prev_score = score_f
       if no_improve > 3000:
           noise_scale = 0.12 if no_improve < 8000 else 0.25
           zz = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-300)) + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n)
           zz = np.where(np.isfinite(zz), zz, 0.0)
           zz -= np.mean(zz)
           z = np.clip(zz, -40.0, 40.0)
           m *= 0.1; v *= 0.1
           tau_cur = min(1.0, tau_cur * 1.6)
           top_cur = min(top_limit, max(top_cur + 32, int(top_cur * 1.6)))
           lr = max(0.002, lr * 0.75)
           no_improve = 0
       if step > 0 and no_improve == 0 and step % 5000 == 0:
           tau_cur = max(0.02, tau_cur * 0.85)
           top_cur = max(32, int(top_cur * 0.8))
           if top_cur > top_limit: top_cur = top_limit
       # choose indices
       tc = int(top_cur)
       if tc >= c_size:
           idx = np.arange(c_size, dtype=np.int64)
       else:
           part = np.argpartition(c, -tc)[-tc:]
           mirror = (c_size - 1) - part
           idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((part, mirror)))
       vals = c[idx]
       maxv = float(vals.max())
       if not np.isfinite(maxv) or maxv <= 0.0:
           break
       tau = tau_cur * maxv
       if tau <= 1e-15:
           w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=float) / float(idx.size)
       else:
           zz = (vals - maxv) / tau
           w = np.exp(np.clip(zz, -50.0, 0.0))
           sw = float(w.sum())
           if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
               w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=float) / float(idx.size)
           else:
               w = w / sw
       if prev_idx.size > 0:
           W[prev_idx] = 0.0
       W[idx] = w
       prev_idx = idx.copy()
       Wref = W[::-1]
       H = np.fft.irfft(P * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)
       grad = 2.0 * H[n-1:c_size][::-1]
       grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
       mean_g = float(np.dot(p, grad))
       grad = grad - p * mean_g
       gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
       if gmax > 0.05:
           grad *= (0.05 / gmax)
       elif gmax <= 1e-15:
           z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
       bias += 1
       m = beta1*m + (1.0-beta1)*grad
       v = beta2*v + (1.0-beta2)*(grad*grad)
       b1 = 1.0 - beta1**bias
       b2 = 1.0 - beta2**bias
       update = (m / b1) / (np.sqrt(v / b2) + eps)
       update = np.where(np.isfinite(update), update, 0.0)
       umax = float(np.max(np.abs(update)))
       if umax > 2.0:
           update *= (2.0 / umax)
       z = z - lr * update
       z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
       z -= np.mean(z)
       z = np.clip(z, -40.0, 40.0)
       p = softmax_z(z)
       step += 1
       if step % 2000 == 0:
           print("active step {} score {:.6f} best {:.6f} top {} tau {:.3f} lr {:.4f}".format(step, score_f, best_s, top_cur, tau_cur, lr))
    final_p = best_p
    if final_p is None: final_p = p
    final_s = fast_score(final_p)
    if not np.isfinite(final_s):
        final_s = best_s
    return final_p, float(final_s)
```
One issue: `b1 = 1 - beta1**bias` if bias large, `beta1**bias` underflows to 0 fine. Recomputing powers each step maybe cost; can use `b1 = 1 - beta1**bias`. Fine. Could maintain incrementally but okay.

In no_improve top_cur tighten: `top_cur = max(32, int(top_cur * 0.8))` but if top_limit smaller? after, if top_cur>top_limit set. If top_limit e.g. 32, okay. If top_cur gets less than 16? max 32. Good.

In pre/long active, `max_top=10**9` top_limit c_size. top_cur starts min(top_k,c_size). If no improve, can reach all.

Potential bug: If `top_cur * 0.8` with top_cur int and top_limit small, int(31*0.8)=24, max(32)=32. Good.

Now, `optimize_fourier_profiles` needs to not access global deadline? It's passed.

Let's code `optimize_fourier_profiles` carefully:
```
def optimize_fourier_profiles(deadline, M=4096, K=4, rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    if M < 256: M=256
    if K <=0: K=2
    freq_base=(2,4,6,8,10,12)
    freqs = freq_base[:max(1,int(K))]
    K = len(freqs)
    fixed = [...]
    starts=[]
    for f in fixed:
       arr=np.zeros(K,dtype=float)
       m=min(K,len(f)); arr[:m]=np.asarray(f[:m],float)
       starts.append(arr)
    a_vals=(-1.0,-0.95,-0.9,-0.85,-0.8,-0.7,-0.6,-0.5)
    b_vals=(-0.3,-0.2,-0.1,0.0,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.4)
    for a in a_vals:
      for b in b_vals:
        arr=np.zeros(K); arr[0]=a; arr[1]=b; starts.append(arr)
    for _ in range(8):
       arr=np.clip(rng.normal(0.0,0.35,K), -1.0,1.0); starts.append(arr)
    best=[]
    for x0 in starts:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       try: s=fourier_score(x0,M,freqs)
       except Exception: s=np.inf
       if np.isfinite(s): best.append((float(s), np.asarray(x0,float).copy()))
    best.sort(key=profile_sort_key)
    if HAVE_MINIMIZE:
       for item in best[:6]:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
          x0=item[1]
          try:
             res=minimize(fourier_score, x0.copy(), args=(M, freqs), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':70,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-7,'adaptive':True})
             xr=np.asarray(res.x,float)
             s2=fourier_score(xr,M,freqs)
             if np.isfinite(s2): best.append((float(s2), xr))
          except Exception:
             pass
    best.sort(key=profile_sort_key)
    out=[]
    for s,x in best:
       if len(out) >= 12: break
       dup=False
       for _,x2 in out:
          if np.allclose(x, x2, rtol=1e-7, atol=1e-8): dup=True; break
       if not dup:
          out.append((float(s), np.asarray(x,float).copy()))
    return out
```
The grid 64 starts, each fourier_score at M=4096. 64*FFT ~ fine. But with Nelder 6*70 evals ~420. Total ~500. Good. Could be 30s.

`fourier_score` with `freqs` as tuple; K=4. If vector length not K? It uses c.size; if c shorter than freqs? In optimize, length K. In make_fourier_seq, frequency list can be longer. Four score clip amplitude to [-1.2,1.2]. If f min negative small, penalty.

Should we normalize f to sum1 before scoring? fast_score normalizes. Penalty if f negative before clip. Good.

Potential issue: For f with min near 0, numerical negative -1e-12, pen 0 due to 1e-8 tolerance. Good.

Now, `make_fourier_seq` with shift: The shift for trigonometric profiles may create f not exactly integral1 after clipping. normalize. Good.

`make_fourier_seq` uses x=np.mod(x,1.0); for edge grid with i=0,x=shift. If shift 0.1, samples not start at 0; okay. If grid='mid', x=(i+0.5)/n + shift; mod.

```
def add_candidate(cands, p, max_len=None): ...
```
No max_len. It normalizes and scores; scoring uses FFT length next pow2. If p length big maybe. Good.

In propose, when adding many candidates, scoring each at N can take time. Limit. For full grid, maybe 36 candidates; fo 6 coeffs shifts 4=24; poly 24. total ~84. Fast score 84*? okay. But if profile optimization already 150s, candidate scoring 1s.

Could avoid scoring duplicate profiles by only adding top unique. Fine.

Now, final code with `max_top=10**9` might be float? int. Good.

Let's consider if active_adam at long phase with `max_top=10**9` and `top_cur` starting 192. We could pass initial top_k=512 or 1024 for long to use many peaks. But top 512 gradient may be noisier? We can set long `top_k=512, tau_rel=0.15, max_top=c_size`. ```python
"""
Improved search for non-negative coefficient sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(i * j sequence) / sum(sequence)^2.

Key ideas in this version:
- Work on the scale-invariant objective with sum(sequence) = 1.
- Seed the search with low-dimensional anti-symmetric trigonometric profiles
  using even sine harmonics, which are strong initial "flat-square" shapes.
- Optimize those profiles rapidly with a small Nelder-Mead pass.
- Refine full-length sequences with an active-set smoothed-max log-Adam loop.
  Only the current hottest convolution coefficients are used for surrogate
  gradients, with top-count / temperature annealing and periodic restarts.
- Keep robust fallbacks and return a clean, non-negative coefficient list.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_MINIMIZE = True
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    HAVE_MINIMIZE = False


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def fast_score(a):
    p = normalize_positive(a)
    if p is None:
        return np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * cm)


def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -50.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def pair_sort_key(item):
    return item[0]


def make_fourier_seq(coeffs, n, freqs, shift=0.0, grid="mid"):
    c = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None

    if grid == "mid":
        x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n) + float(shift)
    else:
        x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)) / float(n) + float(shift)
    x = np.mod(x, 1.0)

    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = min(c.size, len(freqs))
    for j in range(m):
        coeff = float(c[j])
        if abs(coeff) > 1e-14:
            f = f + coeff * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(freqs[j]) * x)

    return normalize_positive(np.maximum(f, 0.0))


def make_poly_seq(coeffs, n):
    c = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    u = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = u
    for coeff in c:
        f = f + float(coeff) * v
        v = v * u * u
    return normalize_positive(np.maximum(f, 0.0))


def fourier_score(vec, M, freqs):
    c = np.asarray(vec, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.clip(c, -1.25, 1.25)
    M = int(M)
    x = (np.arange(M, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(M)
    f = np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64)

    m = min(c.size, len(freqs))
    for j in range(m):
        coeff = float(c[j])
        if abs(coeff) > 1e-14:
            f = f + coeff * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(freqs[j]) * x)

    minf = float(np.min(f))
    penalty = 0.0
    if minf < -1e-8:
        penalty = 200.0 * (minf + 1e-8) * (minf + 1e-8)

    p = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return 1e9
    p = p / s
    sc = fast_score(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e9
    return float(sc + penalty)


def optimize_fourier_profiles(deadline, M=4096, K=4, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    if M < 256:
        M = 256
    if K <= 0:
        K = 2

    freq_base = (2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12)
    freqs = freq_base[:max(1, int(K))]
    K = len(freqs)

    fixed = (
        (-0.70,  0.10,  0.00,  0.00),
        (-0.80,  0.10, -0.05,  0.00),
        (-0.90,  0.12, -0.10,  0.02),
        (-0.95,  0.05, -0.15, -0.02),
        (-1.00,  0.00, -0.20,  0.10),
        (-0.60,  0.25, -0.10,  0.05),
        (-0.50,  0.35, -0.20,  0.15),
        ( 0.50, -0.50,  0.30, -0.15),
        ( 0.70, -0.70,  0.50, -0.25),
        (-0.40,  0.20, -0.40,  0.20),
        (-0.75,  0.05,  0.10, -0.10),
    )

    starts = []
    for f in fixed:
        arr = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(K, len(f))
        arr[:m] = np.asarray(f[:m], dtype=np.float64)
        starts.append(arr)

    a_vals = (-1.00, -0.95, -0.90, -0.85, -0.80, -0.75, -0.70, -0.60, -0.50)
    b_vals = (-0.30, -0.20, -0.10,  0.00,  0.10,  0.20,  0.30,  0.40)
    for a in a_vals:
        for b in b_vals:
            arr = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
            arr[0] = float(a)
            arr[1] = float(b)
            starts.append(arr)

    for _ in range(8):
        arr = np.clip(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, K), -1.0, 1.0).astype(np.float64)
        starts.append(arr)

    best = []
    for x0 in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        try:
            s = fourier_score(x0, M, freqs)
        except Exception:
            s = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(s):
            best.append((float(s), np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()))

    best.sort(key=pair_sort_key)

    if HAVE_MINIMIZE:
        for score0, x0 in best[:6]:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fourier_score,
                    np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy(),
                    args=(M, freqs),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 70,
                        "xatol": 1e-5,
                        "fatol": 1e-7,
                        "adaptive": True
                    }
                )
                xr = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                s2 = fourier_score(xr, M, freqs)
                if np.isfinite(s2):
                    best.append((float(s2), xr))
            except Exception:
                pass

    best.sort(key=pair_sort_key)

    out = []
    for s, x in best:
        if len(out) >= 12:
            break
        dup = False
        for _, x2 in out:
            if np.allclose(x, x2, rtol=1e-7, atol=1e-8):
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            out.append((float(s), np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    return out


def resize_sequence(seq, m):
    p = normalize_positive(seq)
    m = int(m)
    if p is None or m <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, m), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, m))

    n = p.size
    if n == m:
        return p.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return out / s


def compare_update(best_p, best_s, p, s):
    if p is None:
        return best_p, best_s
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return best_p, best_s
    if best_p is None or s < best_s:
        return p, float(s)
    return best_p, best_s


def add_candidate(cands, p):
    p = normalize_positive(p)
    if p is None:
        return
    s = fast_score(p)
    if np.isfinite(s):
        cands.append((float(s), p))


def active_adam(parent, duration_s, rng, top_k=128, tau_rel=0.10, lr0=0.06, max_top=4096):
    p = normalize_positive(parent)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        return p, 2.0

    L = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c_size = 2 * n - 1

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -20.0)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -40.0, 40.0)

    macc = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    vacc = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8
    bias = 0

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_s = fast_score(p)
    no_improve = 0
    step = 0
    lr = float(lr0)
    tau_cur = float(tau_rel)

    top_limit = int(max_top) if max_top is not None else c_size
    if top_limit <= 0:
        top_limit = c_size
    if top_limit > c_size:
        top_limit = c_size
    top_cur = int(min(max(16, int(top_k)), top_limit))

    prev_score = None
    bad = 0

    W = np.zeros(c_size, dtype=np.float64)
    prev_idx = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(duration_s))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:c_size]
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

        cm = float(c.max())
        score_f = float(2.0 * n * cm)

        if np.isfinite(score_f):
            if score_f < best_s - 1e-12:
                best_s = score_f
                best_p = p.copy()
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1

            if prev_score is not None and score_f > prev_score + 1e-12:
                bad += 1
            else:
                bad = 0
            if bad > 200:
                lr = max(0.002, lr * 0.75)
                bad = 0
            prev_score = score_f

        if no_improve > 4000:
            noise_scale = 0.12 if no_improve < 8000 else 0.25
            zz = np.log(np.maximum(best_p, 1e-300)) + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n)
            zz = np.where(np.isfinite(zz), zz, 0.0)
            zz -= float(np.mean(zz))
            z = np.clip(zz, -40.0, 40.0)
            macc *= 0.1
            vacc *= 0.1
            tau_cur = min(1.0, tau_cur * 1.6)
            top_cur = min(top_limit, max(top_cur + 32, int(top_cur * 1.6)))
            lr = max(0.002, lr * 0.75)
            no_improve = 0

        if step > 0 and no_improve == 0 and step % 5000 == 0:
            tau_cur = max(0.02, tau_cur * 0.85)
            top_cur = max(32, int(top_cur * 0.8))
            if top_cur > top_limit:
                top_cur = top_limit

        tc = int(top_cur)
        if tc >= c_size:
            idx = np.arange(c_size, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            part = np.argpartition(c, -tc)[-tc:]
            mirror = (c_size - 1) - part
            idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((part, mirror)))

        vals = c[idx]
        maxv = float(vals.max())
        if not np.isfinite(maxv) or maxv <= 0.0:
            break

        tau = tau_cur * maxv
        if tau <= 1e-15:
            w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(idx.size)
        else:
            zz = (vals - maxv) / tau
            w = np.exp(np.clip(zz, -50.0, 0.0))
            sw = float(w.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
                w = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(idx.size)
            else:
                w = w / sw

        if prev_idx.size > 0:
            W[prev_idx] = 0.0
        W[idx] = w
        prev_idx = idx.copy()

        Wref = W[::-1]
        H = np.fft.irfft(P * np.fft.rfft(Wref, L), L)
        grad = 2.0 * H[n - 1:c_size][::-1]
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

        mean_g = float(np.dot(p, grad))
        grad = grad - p * mean_g

        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if gmax > 0.05:
            grad *= (0.05 / gmax)
        elif gmax <= 1e-15:
            z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)

        bias += 1
        macc = beta1 * macc + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
        vacc = beta2 * vacc + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
        b1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** bias
        b2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** bias
        update = (macc / b1) / (np.sqrt(vacc / b2) + eps)
        update = np.where(np.isfinite(update), update, 0.0)

        umax = float(np.max(np.abs(update)))
        if umax > 2.0:
            update *= (2.0 / umax)

        z = z - lr * update
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        z = np.clip(z, -40.0, 40.0)
        p = softmax_z(z)

        step += 1
        if step % 2000 == 0:
            print(
                "active step {} score {:.6f} best {:.6f} top {} tau {:.3f} lr {:.4f}".format(
                    step, score_f, best_s, top_cur, tau_cur, lr
                )
            )

    final_p = best_p
    if final_p is None:
        final_p = p
    final_s = fast_score(final_p)
    if not np.isfinite(final_s):
        final_s = best_s
    return final_p, float(final_s)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        if budget_s is None:
            budget = float(kwargs.get("budget_s", 2380))
        else:
            budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2380.0

    budget = max(60.0, float(budget))
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(30.0, budget - 30.0)

    if budget < 600.0:
        N = 4096
    else:
        N = 8192

    cands = []

    # Robust baseline.
    add_candidate(cands, np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N))

    # Try to continue from the previous construction if available.
    try:
        prev = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        prev = np.where(np.isfinite(prev), prev, 0.0)
        prev = np.maximum(prev, 0.0)
        if prev.size > 2:
            add_candidate(cands, resize_sequence(prev, N))
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Low-dimensional Fourier profile discovery.
    fo_deadline = min(deadline, start_time + max(30.0, min(180.0, budget * 0.08)))
    fo_freqs = (2, 4, 6, 8)
    try:
        fo_profiles = optimize_fourier_profiles(fo_deadline, M=4096, K=4, rng=rng)
    except Exception:
        fo_profiles = []

    # Add optimized Fourier profiles, with a few phase shifts.
    for _, coeffs in fo_profiles[:7]:
        for shift in (0.0, 0.08, -0.08):
            p = make_fourier_seq(coeffs, N, fo_freqs, shift=shift, grid="mid")
            add_candidate(cands, p)
        p = make_fourier_seq(coeffs, N, fo_freqs, shift=0.0, grid="edge")
        add_candidate(cands, p)

    # Direct coarse two-harmonic grid in the full-length domain.
    for a in (-1.00, -0.90, -0.80, -0.70, -0.60, -0.50):
        for b in (-0.20, -0.10, 0.00, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30):
            coeffs = np.array([a, b, 0.0, 0.0], dtype=np.float64)
            p = make_fourier_seq(coeffs, N, fo_freqs, shift=0.0, grid="mid")
            add_candidate(cands, p)

    # A few non-periodic antisymmetric polynomial profiles for diversity.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
        for _ in range(24):
            d = int(rng.integers(2, 5))
            cc = rng.uniform(-0.45, 0.45, d)
            p = make_poly_seq(cc, N)
            add_candidate(cands, p)

    if not cands:
        add_candidate(cands, np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N))

    cands.sort(key=pair_sort_key)
    best_p = cands[0][1]
    best_s = cands[0][0]

    # Choose a few distinct promising parents for early refinement.
    parents = []
    for s, p in cands:
        if len(parents) >= 3:
            break
        dup = False
        for q in parents:
            if np.allclose(p, q, rtol=1e-6, atol=1e-8):
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            parents.append(p)
    if not parents:
        parents.append(best_p)

    # Multi-start active refinement.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 90.0:
        per = min(120.0, max(30.0, rem * 0.12))
        for p0 in parents:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 30.0:
                break
            dur = min(per, rem * 0.5)
            if dur < 10.0:
                continue
            try:
                p1, s1 = active_adam(
                    p0,
                    dur,
                    rng,
                    top_k=96,
                    tau_rel=0.10,
                    lr0=0.08,
                    max_top=2048
                )
                best_p, best_s = compare_update(best_p, best_s, p1, s1)
                print("parent polish best {:.6f}".format(best_s))
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Long active-set polish from the best state.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        try:
            p2, s2 = active_adam(
                best_p,
                rem * 0.95,
                rng,
                top_k=512,
                tau_rel=0.15,
                lr0=0.05,
                max_top=10 ** 9
            )
            best_p, best_s = compare_update(best_p, best_s, p2, s2)
            print("long polish best {:.6f}".format(best_s))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Optional larger-length interpolation only if it gives a clear gain.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 900.0 and best_s > 1.50:
        try:
            big = resize_sequence(best_p, 2 * N)
            big_s = fast_score(big)
            if np.isfinite(big_s) and big_s < best_s - 1e-4:
                if rem > 1500.0:
                    dur = min(300.0, rem - 150.0)
                    big2, big2_s = active_adam(
                        big,
                        dur,
                        rng,
                        top_k=256,
                        tau_rel=0.12,
                        lr0=0.04,
                        max_top=2048
                    )
                    big, big_s = compare_update(big, big_s, big2, big2_s)
                best_p, best_s = compare_update(best_p, best_s, big, big_s)
                print("larger sequence best {:.6f} length {}".format(best_s, best_p.size))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final cleanup and return.
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    seq = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64)
    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if seq.size == 0 or float(seq.sum()) < 0.01:
        seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    return [float(x) for x in seq]
```
